========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I suppose... they might just see what those few people in the ensemble want them to see. They always have these emails or notes they show me that supposedly explain it all away. So maybe an outsider would just believe their excuses. 

But... I've been dealing with this for almost two years, ever since the commission. I just can't shake the certainty about what they're doing. If someone was actually paying attention, they'd see how the schedule changes, the seating choices, the little comments in rehearsals... it's all set up to quietly undermine my work. I don't really care how someone else might see it, honestly. I just know what I'm experiencing.
